$s(t)=\sum_{i}A_{i}\sin(\underbrace{ \omega_{i} }_{ 2\pi F }\varphi_{i})$

### ex 1
$s(t)=10\sin(\underbrace{ 2\pi \overbrace{ 50 }^{ 50Hz } }_{ \omega }·t)$

### ex 2
$s(t)=10\sin(2\pi·100t)+2\sin(2\pi·400t)+1$

$A_{1}=10$
$\omega_{1}=2\pi·100$
$\varphi_{1}=0$

$A_{2}=2$
$\omega_{2}=2\pi·400$
$\varphi_{2}=0$

$A_{0}=1$
$\omega_{0}=0$
$\varphi=90\degree$
